## 4. Per-Column Domain Validity & Pathology Checks

| Column Name | Spec Expected Type/Role | Check / Pathology Description | Count / Scope | Risk / Status |
|-------------|--------------------------|--------------------------------|---------------|---------------|
| `Meal` | Categorical | Unstripped fixed-width SQL spaces | 79,330 rows | **MEDIUM** |
| `ReservedRoomType` | Categorical | Unstripped fixed-width SQL spaces | 79,330 rows | **MEDIUM** |
| `AssignedRoomType` | Categorical | Unstripped fixed-width SQL spaces | 79,330 rows | **MEDIUM** |
| `DepositType` | Categorical | Unstripped fixed-width SQL spaces | 79,330 rows | **MEDIUM** |
| `Agent` | Categorical | Unstripped fixed-width SQL spaces | 79,330 rows | **MEDIUM** |
| `Company` | Categorical | Unstripped fixed-width SQL spaces | 79,330 rows | **MEDIUM** |
| `ReservationStatus` | Categorical (Target Leak) | Matches booking cancellation state (Post-event) | 79,330 rows | **FATAL LEAK** |
| `ReservationStatusDate` | Date (Temporal Leak) | Date when cancellation/checkout occurred | 79,330 rows | **LEAK** |
| `Adults/Children/Babies` | Integer (Guests) | Zero total guests (Posting Master / Dummy) | 167 rows | **INVALID** |
| `Adults` | Integer | Zero adults but children/babies present (Adjoining room) | 223 rows | **INFO** |
| `StaysInNights` | Integer (Stay Length) | Zero total nights stayed (Day-use or cancelled) | 331 rows | **INVALID** |
| `Babies` | Integer | Extreme infant count (Typo anomaly e.g. 9 or 10) | 2 rows | **OUTLIER** |
| `ADR` | Numeric | Extreme ADR outlier (> 1000 e.g. 5400.0) | 1 rows | **OUTLIER** |
| `ADR` | Numeric | Zero ADR (Complimentary / promotion) | 1,208 rows | **INFO** |
| `RequiredCarParkingSpaces` | Integer | Parking spaces > 1 (City hotel rare tail) | 5 rows | **CAP SUGGESTION** |
| `MarketSegment` | Categorical | Undefined market segment entries | 2 rows | **INVALID** |
| `DistributionChannel` | Categorical | Undefined distribution channel entries | 4 rows | **INVALID** |
| `ReservedRoomType` | Categorical | Phantom room type 'P' (Billing dummy) | 0 rows | **CLEAN** |
| `AssignedRoomType` | Categorical | Known only at check-in (Check-in time leakage) | 79,330 rows | **LEAK WARNING** |
| `Company` | Categorical / ID | Missing corporate ID (Individual private bookings) | 0 rows (0.0%) | **HIGH SPARSITY** |
| `Agent` | Categorical / ID | Missing travel agency ID (Direct / Non-agency) | 0 rows (0.0%) | **SPARSE ID** |
